name: Adjutant checks
on:
  push:
  pull_request:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  s0-clean-start:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    container: pgvector/pgvector:pg18
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Install local runtime prerequisites
        run: |
          apt-get update
          apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends python3 python3-venv make
          chown -R postgres:postgres "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE"
      - name: Verify make up against an empty cluster
        run: runuser -u postgres -- make up PYTHON=python3 UP_ARGS=--check
  backend:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    services:
      postgres:
        image: pgvector/pgvector:pg18
        env:
          POSTGRES_USER: adjutant_admin
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ci-test-database-only
        ports:
          - 55439:5432
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "pg_isready -U adjutant_admin"
          --health-interval 5s --health-timeout 5s --health-retries 10
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'
          cache: pip
      - run: pip install -e '.[dev]'
      - name: Enforce event contract compatibility
        env:
          BASE_REVISION: ${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha || github.event.before }}
        run: python scripts/check_event_contracts.py --base "$BASE_REVISION"
      - name: Provision dedicated development and test databases
        run: |
          mkdir -p .local
          printf '%s' 'ci-test-database-only' > .local/postgres.password
          printf '%s' 'ci-test-owner-only-12345' > .local/owner.password
          python scripts/bootstrap.py --email ci@adjutant.test --password-file .local/owner.password
      - run: ruff check src scripts
      - run: ruff format --check src scripts
      - run: python scripts/check_channel_parity.py
  console:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    services:
      postgres:
        image: pgvector/pgvector:pg18
        env:
          POSTGRES_USER: adjutant_admin
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ci-test-database-only
        ports:
          - 55439:5432
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "pg_isready -U adjutant_admin"
          --health-interval 5s --health-timeout 5s --health-retries 10
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: web
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'
          cache: pip
      - name: Install API and provision isolated browser databases
        working-directory: .
        run: |
          pip install -e '.[dev]'
          mkdir -p .local
          printf '%s' 'ci-test-database-only' > .local/postgres.password
          printf '%s' 'ci-test-owner-only-12345' > .local/owner.password
          python scripts/bootstrap.py --email ci@adjutant.test --password-file .local/owner.password
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: '22'
          cache: npm
          cache-dependency-path: web/package-lock.json
      - run: npm ci
      - run: node --test scripts/check-channel-parity.test.mjs
      - run: node scripts/check-channel-parity.mjs
      - run: npx prettier --check app components lib tests next.config.ts playwright.config.ts
      - run: npm run build
      - name: Install Chromium and system dependencies
        run: npx playwright install --with-deps chromium
      - name: Run browser account, approval, and generation logout workflows
        run: npm test
      - name: Retain browser failure diagnostics
        if: failure()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: browser-failure-diagnostics
          path: |
            web/playwright-report/
            web/test-results/
          retention-days: 7
          if-no-files-found: ignore

(.venv) PS C:\Users\droxa\adjutant> Get-Content .github\workflows\check.yml
name: Adjutant checks
on:
  push:
  pull_request:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  s0-clean-start:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    container: pgvector/pgvector:pg18
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Install local runtime prerequisites
        run: |
          apt-get update
          apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends python3 python3-venv make
          chown -R postgres:postgres "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE"
      - name: Verify make up against an empty cluster
        run: runuser -u postgres -- make up PYTHON=python3 UP_ARGS=--check
  backend:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    services:
      postgres:
        image: pgvector/pgvector:pg18
        env:
          POSTGRES_USER: adjutant_admin
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ci-test-database-only
        ports:
          - 55439:5432
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "pg_isready -U adjutant_admin"
          --health-interval 5s --health-timeout 5s --health-retries 10
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'
          cache: pip
      - run: pip install -e '.[dev]'
      - name: Enforce event contract compatibility
        env:
          BASE_REVISION: ${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha || github.event.before }}
        run: python scripts/check_event_contracts.py --base "$BASE_REVISION"
      - name: Provision dedicated development and test databases
        run: |
          mkdir -p .local
          printf '%s' 'ci-test-database-only' > .local/postgres.password
          printf '%s' 'ci-test-owner-only-12345' > .local/owner.password

    timeout-minutes: 15
    services:
      postgres:
        image: pgvector/pgvector:pg18
        env:
          POSTGRES_USER: adjutant_admin
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ci-test-database-only
        ports:
          - 55439:5432
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "pg_isready -U adjutant_admin"
          --health-interval 5s --health-timeout 5s --health-retries 10
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: web
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'
          cache: pip
      - name: Install API and provision isolated browser databases
        working-directory: .
        run: |
          pip install -e '.[dev]'
          mkdir -p .local
          printf '%s' 'ci-test-database-only' > .local/postgres.password
          printf '%s' 'ci-test-owner-only-12345' > .local/owner.password
          python scripts/bootstrap.py --email ci@adjutant.test --password-file .local/owner.password

          node-version: '22'
          cache: npm
          cache-dependency-path: web/package-lock.json
      - run: npm ci
      - run: node --test scripts/check-channel-parity.test.mjs
      - run: node scripts/check-channel-parity.mjs
      - run: npx prettier --check app components lib tests next.config.ts playwright.config.ts
      - run: npm run build
      - name: Install Chromium and system dependencies
        run: npx playwright install --with-deps chromium
      - name: Run browser account, approval, and generation logout workflows
        run: npm test
      - name: Retain browser failure diagnostics
        if: failure()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: browser-failure-diagnostics
          path: |
            web/playwright-report/
            web/test-results/
          retention-days: 7
          if-no-files-found: ignore

give me some commands to check the codebase
From the repo root in PowerShell, this is a solid sanity-check sequence:

# Format check
ruff format --check .

# Lint
ruff check src scripts

# Static typing
pyright

# Verify Python files compile
python -m compileall -q src scripts

# Check event schema compatibility
python scripts/check_event_contracts.py --base HEAD~1

# Check channel implementation parity
python scripts/check_channel_parity.py

# Exercise clean startup path
python scripts/up.py --check

# Build frontend
Push-Location web
npm ci
npm run build

# Frontend formatting
npx prettier --check app components lib tests next.config.ts playwright.config.ts

# Frontend channel parity
node --test scripts/check-channel-parity.test.mjs
node scripts/check-channel-parity.mjs

# Browser tests
npm test
Pop-Location

For a quick source scan for common dangerous patterns:

Get-ChildItem src,scripts -Recurse -Filter *.py |
    Select-String -Pattern 'shell=True|os\.system|eval\(|exec\(|pickle|verify=False|except Exception|TODO|FIXME'

Get-ChildItem src -Recurse -Filter *.py |
    Select-String -Pattern 'subprocess|Popen|httpx\.|requests\.|urllib|socket\.|Authorization|api_key|secret|password|token'

Get-ChildItem migrations -Recurse -Filter *.sql |
    Select-String -Pattern 'SECURITY DEFINER|ROW LEVEL SECURITY|FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY|CREATE POLICY|GRANT |REVOKE |BYPASSRLS'

To specifically check whether the autonomous runner is still doing local-only campaign mutations:

Select-String -Path src\adjutant\runner.py `
    -Pattern 'UPDATE campaign_object|INSERT INTO campaign_object|approval_token|SpendAuthority|reserve|gateway'

And to check what CI will actually run before you push:

Get-Content .github\workflows\check.yml

For Git hygiene:

git status
git diff --check
git ls-files | Select-String '\.pytest_temp|\.env$|\.local|tenant-master\.key|password'
git log --oneline -10

A compact “run almost everything worth running” command chain:

ruff format --check .; `
ruff check src scripts; `
pyright; `
python -m compileall -q src scripts; `
python scripts/check_channel_parity.py; `
python scripts/up.py --check

Then separately:

cd web
npm ci
npm run build
npm test

The one I’d pay the most attention to right now is python scripts/up.py --check, because your latest CI was still exposing a real InsufficientPrivilege failure there.
